Consequence: views hold about 3 to 5 elements per rank, and every large view is split per functional group, as AC-5 requires.'
        - 'Entity views are generated, not hand-drawn: one element per entity across all ER files (D4) with every FK relationship covered by at least one generated view. Consequence: regenerating after an EF model change keeps the diagrams honest.'
        - 'Static C4 views are generated neighbourhood views, not hand-drawn overviews: at the fixed 0.635 scale a page holds a hub and about three neighbours, so the only way to show every element and relationship at 8 to 16 pt is a covering set of small views. Consequence: 174 C4 views with a coverage check, and the generator and hub sizes are kept as D9 tools for regeneration.'
        - 'Layout follows measurement, not estimate: left to right made labels widen ranks (45 overflows), top to bottom with 50 between siblings brought it to 24, and the render log fed back smaller groups until 0.
